HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B005","root_000409/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_06f0230d047f0d32573f","text":"5. **The pupil-image inside darkness.** Lexical evidence: `ء ن س` B005, the little human image in the pupil; `خ س ر` B001/B005, diminution and ruin/baseness. Activated by `لَفِي`, the inward location, and by `ح ق ق` B011, fitted socket or exact middle, plus `ص ب ر` B004, sides or brim. Structural cue: several branches create container/interior imagery: in loss, socket, sides, brim. Assigned role: `الإنسان` is a small reflected figure inside a dark center; `خسر` is the encompassing interior darkness or diminution. The packet supplies the eye-image and container-like branches. I infer that the spatial grammar allows these images to resonate. Reading change: this is exploratory, but it makes the verse visually interior: the human appears as a small image enclosed within loss, awaiting the later stabilizing frame of truth and patience.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000347/B001","root_000409/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2ee5f65577b29f4813e6","text":"3. **Loss as short measure, corrected by truth.** Lexical evidence: `خ س ر` B003, shorting measure and weight; activated by `ح ق ق` B001/B005, firm truth and making truth stand, and `ع م ل` B005, transactions between people. Structural cue: the later phrase `بالحق` follows a repeated act of mutual enjoining, suggesting correction within exchange. Assigned role: `خسر` is not only suffered loss but also deficient measuring; `الحق` becomes the standard that restores measure. The packet supplies the short-measure branch and truth/verification branches. I infer that advice \"to truth\" functions as recalibration of the deficient scale. Reading change: the human condition can be read as being inside a regime of under-measurement: one loses, and perhaps causes loss, until truth is made the measure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B001","root_000059/B001","root_000059/B002","root_000347/B003","root_000409/B001","root_000409/B002","root_000840/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3db68ebbe6dd43592dc7","text":"1. The human as visible social presence caught in recurring diminution. Lexical evidence: fixed `ء ن س B001` (visible human presence over against hidden/wild beings) and `خ س ر B001` (general deficiency), activated by `ع ص ر B001` (recurring time) and `ر ح م B002` (kinship bond). Structural cue: the statement is emphatic and generic, then is limited by a plural exception in 103:3. Assigned role: `الإنسان` names humanity as a visible social species, while `خسر` names the erosion that time reveals in that species. Abductive move: I supply that the plural exception retroactively marks the singular generic human as a collective condition, not merely an isolated individual. Reading change: the ayah becomes a diagnosis of the human creature as publicly exposed to depletion unless a bond-system interrupts it. 2. Loss as commercial and metrological deficit. Lexical evidence: fixed `خ س ر B002` (trade loss) and `خ س ر B003` (shorting measure/weight), activated by `ع م ل B004` (wage/pay for work), `ع م ل B005` (dealing or transaction), `ح ق ق B002` (due obligation), and `ح ق ق B003` (particular owned right). Structural cue: loss is followed by work, right, and reciprocal enjoining; the exception reads like a correction of accounts. Assigned role: `خسر` supplies the deficit ledger; work and right supply the means of making accounts answer to what is due. Abductive move: I infer that loss is not only suffered but produced by failed measure, failed profit, or unpaid due. Reading change: `لَفِي خُسْرٍ` reads as being inside an unbalanced economy of existence, where capital, measure, and rights are all at risk. 3. Loss as damaged perception and estranged companionship. Lexical evidence: fixed `ء ن س B002` (perceiving into awareness by sight, hearing, or sensation), `ء ن س B003` (comfort that removes estrangement), and `خ س ر B005` (loss, ruin, baseness, weakness), activated by `ء م ن B001` (settled security/trust), `و ص ي B003` (mutual exhortation), and `ص ب ر B003` (standing with someone). Structural cue: the next ayah does not only say “do good”; it creates a reciprocal speaking-and-standing structure. Assigned role: `الإنسان` can be the perceiving/companionable being; `خسر` is the collapse of that awareness and companionship into weakness. Abductive move: I supply the relational psychology: a creature made for awareness and familiar comfort is “in loss” when those capacities fail or turn lonely. Reading change: the loss becomes existential and social estrangement, not just moral failure or negative balance. 4. Loss as enclosure under pressure. Lexical evidence: fixed `خ س ر B001` (diminution) with the phrase `لَفِي` as spatial cue, activated by `ع ص ر B002` (pressing until liquid runs), `ع ص ر B006` (withholding/holding back), `ص ب ر B002` (compelled holding), and `ص ب ر B006` (inescapable trouble). Structural cue: the ayah places the human “in” loss after the oath by `العصر`, then 103:3 describes a way of holding together. Assigned role: `خسر` becomes the enclosing medium; `العصر` supplies pressure; `صبر` supplies counter-holding. Abductive move: I infer that the preposition makes loss a containing condition, while the surrounding roots specify whether enclosure destroys or disciplines. Reading change: the ayah reads as a human being trapped in compressive diminishment, awaiting a different form of containment in patience and mutual charge.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000409/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_462d302c55243de992b7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B001\"]],[\"103:2:4\",\"root_000409\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İnsan burada yalnız bir birey değil, zaman içinde eksilmesi herkesçe görünür hale gelen toplumsal bir varlık olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_52a7c02fe34e11ea2bc5","text":"1. **Humanity inside a loss-account.** Lexical evidence: `ء ن س` B001, visible human presence, and `خ س ر` B001/B002, general deficiency and trade loss. Activated by `ع ص ر` B001/B002 in 103:1 and by `ع م ل` B004/B005 plus `ح ق ق` B003 in 103:3. Structural cue: `لَفِي` gives spatial immersion, while the surrounding ayat create an accounting sequence: oath, deficit, exception. Assigned role: `الإنسان` is the visible human subject entered into the account; `خسر` is the negative balance. The packet supplies human presence, loss, work/pay/transaction, and right/claim. I infer the accounting frame from their adjacency. Reading change: the ayah reads not only as a universal moral claim but as a balance-sheet verdict: the human is already located inside diminishing capital.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5b2be38f4e34b9bddf51","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:4\",\"root_000409\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kayıp, zaman harcandıkça büyüyen, sermayeyi koruyamayan ve karşılık üretemeyen zararlı bir işlem boyutu kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5b6aa4cc853ef27ff1d9","text":"5. **Loss includes estrangement from the human social capacity.** **Lexical evidence:** ء ن س `B003` (companionship that removes estrangement), activated by ص ل ح `B002` (removing interpersonal hostility), و ص ي `B001`/`B003` (joining through mutual charge), and ص ب ر `B003` (standing with others). **Structural cue and role:** the generic singular `الإنسان` is enclosed in loss, but the exception shifts to a plural whose defining verbs include two reciprocal forms; companionship supplies a dormant human capacity, and reciprocal repair makes it operative. **Abductive move:** I infer that isolated self-sufficiency is part of the loss and that social attachment helps reverse it. **Reading change:** the statement diagnoses not only depleted individuals but a failure of human togetherness, with escape taking a necessarily communal form.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5c833105e814bdffe020","text":"1. **Loss as a specified, remediable deficit.** **Lexical evidence:** خ س ر `B001` (general deficiency), sharpened by ص ل ح `B001` (soundness against corruption) and the opening ر ح م `B001` (beneficence). **Structural cue and role:** the emphatic `إن ... لفي` encloses the generic human in deficiency, but `إلا` immediately identifies a four-part exception; deficiency supplies the diagnosed lack, and the exception supplies its missing capacities. **Abductive move:** I infer from the logical complement that trust, sound action, truth-bearing reciprocity, and restraint are what the deficient condition lacks. **Reading change:** `خسر` reads less as an unexplained terminal ruin and more as a severe but diagnostically articulated shortfall disclosed under a mercy-frame.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6d2a18a4d2285111e07c","text":"2. **A losing bargain under temporal pressure.** **Lexical evidence:** خ س ر `B002` (trade loss), activated by ع ص ر `B002` (pressing), ع ص ر `B007` (extracted gain), ع م ل `B004` (wage), and ع م ل `B005` (dealing between people). **Structural cue and role:** the oath names a process that can extract yield, `خسر` names failure to preserve capital or profit, and the exception names intentional labor and dealings that can produce a return. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies the commercial and productive branches; I supply the equation of finite time with capital and of the listed practices with return. **Reading change:** the human is not merely unfortunate but already situated inside an unprofitable transaction whose losses accrue as time is spent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_000409/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6ea3302d9c7bd63eb102","text":"Ayah 103:3 changed the scope of both nouns. `الحق` as due obligation/right activated خ س ر `B003`, so loss became social short-measuring as well as general deficiency or failed profit. The shift from generic singular `الإنسان` to plural actors twice counseling one another activated ء ن س `B002`/`B003`: a human's loss may be hard to perceive alone and may include estrangement itself. This later exception also corrected any terminal reading of `خسر`; it specifies a demanding communal remedy without weakening the emphatic diagnosis.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_81f600a176d2cd700d0d","text":"3. **Loss as giving less than is due.** **Lexical evidence:** خ س ر `B003` (shorting measure and weight), activated by ح ق ق `B002` (due obligation), `B003` (an owned right), ص ل ح `B002` (reconciliation), and ع م ل `B005` (human transaction). **Structural cue and role:** 103:3 places `الحق` inside doubled reciprocal counsel after sound acts; short measure supplies the social fault, due right the standard, reconciliation the repair, and mutual counsel the checking mechanism. **Abductive move:** I infer that at least one cause of the universal deficit is systematic under-delivery to reality and to other people; the packet gives no explicit scale or debtor. **Reading change:** `في خسر` acquires an ethical and legal edge: the human inhabits a regime of short measure, not only a private state of disappointment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9124d4225946fe93e59c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:4\",\"root_000409\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kayıp ayrıca, başkalarına ve gerçeğe verilmesi gerekeni eksik ölçme, hakkı teslim etmeme düzenini anlatır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000347/B002","root_000409/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_93792a458e219f9f630e","text":"103:3 made the singular `الإنسان` feel less solitary than it first appeared: the plural exception and doubled `تَوَاصَوْا` retroactively show that the human loss is repaired, if at all, in a reciprocal field. The biggest reading change came from `خ س ر B003`; before the final ayah it could remain generic loss, but `ح ق ق B002/B003` made loss look like shorted measure and unpaid right. The final `صبر` also altered the spatial force of `لَفِي`: being “in loss” was answered not by leaving enclosure altogether, but by entering a different disciplined holding.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_98ff1d0d1a994859bcc7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"İnsanın durumu, kendi gözünden kaçanı bir başkasının bakışında gösteren ayna benzeri bir karşılaşmayla görünür olabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002","root_000347/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a14f3b2df6d69d2127f0","text":"4. **The perceiver needs another perceiver to expose the enclosure.** **Lexical evidence:** ء ن س `B002` (detecting by sight, hearing, or inward sensation) and, exploratorily, `B005` (the human image in another's pupil), activated by و ص ي `B003` (mutual exhortation) and ح ق ق `B005` (making truth stand). **Structural cue and role:** the one capable of awareness is nevertheless `في` loss, while the exception uses reciprocal speech; perception supplies latent capacity, another person's voice or gaze supplies external correction, and truth supplies what becomes visible. **Abductive move:** I infer that enclosure distorts self-assessment and that mutual counsel functions as a social sensor or mirror. **Reading change:** `الإنسان` reads as a perceiving being whose own deficit may become perceptible only through another human's truthful intervention.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ad170aaf62d24da31cc1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Algılayabilen insanın kendi kaybını fark etmesi, başka bir insanın doğru sözle yaptığı uyarıya ihtiyaç duyabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b6f61c467a87ba2183a5","text":"103:3’s repeated `تواصوا` surprised the reading of `الإنسان`: the active opposite of loss is not solitary excellence but reciprocal transmission, so `ء ن س` B003/B006 became central. `بالحق` also pushed `خسر` toward B003, short measure, because truth functions like the correcting standard. `بالصبر` then added a collective-liability shade through `ص ب ر` B003, suretyship: the human in loss is not just individually deficient but needs others to stand with him under obligation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003","root_000409/B001","root_001656/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bb57ecc768be0466c2d9","text":"2. **The social creature loses when separated from reciprocal repair.** Lexical evidence: `ء ن س` B003/B006, familiarity, companionship, intimate associate; `خ س ر` B001, diminution. Activated by `و ص ي` B003 twice, mutual exhortation, and `ص ل ح` B002, reconciliation between people. Structural cue: 103:2 names `الإنسان` singularly/generically, while 103:3 answers with a plural community that mutually charges one another. Assigned role: `الإنسان` is not merely biological humankind but the being made for familiar nearness; `خسر` is the diminishment of that being when the reciprocal social circuit is absent. The packet supplies the companionship branches and the repeated reciprocal form. I infer that the plural exception repairs the generic human’s isolation. Reading change: the verse is sharpened from \"man is in loss\" to \"the human-as-social-being is in loss unless rejoined to a mutual moral companionship.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B003","root_000409/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d2f319a017bc4bc9407b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:2\",\"root_000059\",[\"B003\"]],[\"103:2:4\",\"root_000409\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kayıp, insanı başkalarıyla yakınlık kurma kapasitesinden uzaklaştıran ve zayıflatan bir yabancılaşmayı da içerir; çıkış bu yüzden karşılıklı dayanışmayla toplumsaldır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000409/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e86f91767300c4eb12ea","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"103:2:4\",\"root_000409\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kayıp, insanı baskı altında kuşatan bir ortam gibi işler; sabır ve karşılıklı öğüt buna karşı farklı bir tutunma alanı açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000054/B002","root_000059/B002","root_000409/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f1f076cdf56ab0db3d67","text":"4. **The perceiving human fails to perceive the deficit.** Lexical evidence: `ء ن س` B002, perceiving by seeing, sensing, or hearing; `خ س ر` B001, deficiency. Activated by `ء م ن` B002, confirming a report or promise, and `ح ق ق` B001, reality/certainty. Structural cue: 103:2 is asserted with `إن` and `لـ`, while the repair in 103:3 includes assent and truth. Assigned role: `الإنسان` is the one capable of sensing and recognizing; `خسر` is the condition that must be recognized, not merely endured. The packet supplies perception, confirmation, and truth. I infer that the emphatic assertion confronts a failure of awareness.
